[CASE:LOOK_OUTSIDE]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]
Look Outside [Francis Coulombe -- RPG Maker horror; 2024]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: ONTOLOGICAL INCOMPATIBILITY HARM [new framework
    category -- harm produced by proximity to incompatible
    existence rather than hostile intent]; DENIAL AS SUBSTRATE
    vs DENIAL AS SURFACE [named distinction]; RITUAL AS
    CONSCIOUSNESS-TRANSFER MECHANISM [not protection but
    awareness-creation]; FORCED TRANSFORMATION as terminal
    outcome distinct from death; VOLUNTARY AMNESIA as
    survival mechanism post-ritual-truth; CURIOSITY OVERRIDE
    [instrument cannot stop at safe ignorance when substrate
    is already known to exist]; DENIAL RELEASE [removing
    amnesia surface does not restore human instrument --
    releases meat-substrate]; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY as direct
    damage vector; C2 RESTORATION FOR THE THREAT
    ACTOR rather than for the instrument.

  COSMOLOGICAL STRUCTURE [confirmed by wiki]:
    The Visitor is a vast entity that has never encountered
    another being capable of observing things. It thought
    Earth was a part of its own body it had lost track of.
    Sybil [astronomer] noticed a patch of stars disappearing
    -- the Visitor -- and observed it through her telescope.
    Her observation was what caused the Visitor to notice
    Earth and approach to investigate. She is always taken:
    the origin event is Sybil looking, which starts
    everything, and she is taken in multiple endings.
    The Visitor's presence near Earth causes automatic
    mutation and mind destruction in anyone exposed to it.
    Not hostility -- the Visitor had no prior awareness of
    causing harm. The damage is ontological: proximity to
    what the Visitor is does something to human instruments
    automatically, without intent, without the Visitor's
    knowledge until the ritual creates that knowledge.
    15 days: if no ritual, the Visitor moves on regardless.
    The cosmological ending is not resolved by the
    instrument -- it resolves on the Visitor's own schedule.

  ONTOLOGICAL INCOMPATIBILITY HARM [new category]:
    The Visitor causes harm automatically and unconsciously.
    It is not hostile. It is not indifferent. It simply
    exists in a way that is incompatible with human
    instruments surviving proximity in their current form.
    This is structurally distinct from every extraction mechanism in
    the framework index: extraction mechanisms are deployed intentionally.
    The Visitor's effect is the automatic consequence of
    what it is in relation to what humans are.
    The correct counter is not a extraction mechanism counter. It is
    creating the conditions for the thing causing harm to
    become aware that it is causing harm -- which it
    structurally cannot be without those conditions.

  RITUAL AS CONSCIOUSNESS-TRANSFER MECHANISM:
    The ritual allows contact between the instrument and
    the Visitor. Through this contact, the Visitor becomes
    self-aware for the first time -- aware that it is a
    separate being from Earth, aware that it is causing
    harm to the instruments it encounters. By showing the
    Visitor humanity's depictions of it, the player
    establishes that humans are independent beings, not
    lost fragments of the Visitor's body.
    The ritual doesn't protect the instrument from the
    Visitor. It gives the Visitor the MI it lacked: the
    Visitor's CC was present [it will leave once aware]
    but unavailable without the awareness the ritual
    creates. Ritual restores MI to the Visitor; Visitor's
    CC fires; Visitor leaves.
    Cost: the ritual always changes the body [horror body].
    The body cost is the price of creating the conditions
    for Visitor awareness. Whether mind survives depends
    on whether the instrument looks at the Visitor's
    true form during contact.

  ENDING TYPOLOGY [confirmed]:

    SAFE IGNORANCE / NO GOING BACK:
      Denial holds for 15 days. Visitor moves on regardless.
      Instrument survives intact. Body fine, mind fine.
      "No going back" = the instrument now knows the thing
      existed and was survived, which cannot be unknown.
      The innocence of not-knowing is permanently gone even
      though the eyes never opened. The Visitor leaving on
      its own schedule means safe ignorance and successful
      ritual can look identical from the instrument's
      perspective -- in both cases the Visitor is gone.
      Best survivable outcome.

    RITUAL DENIAL [horror body, mind intact]:
      Ritual used to maintain not-looking. The body is
      changed by the ritual contact [horror body] but
      the mind survives intact. The ritual's presence
      near the Visitor costs the body regardless of
      whether the instrument looks. Mind preserved.
      Better than ritual truth; worse than safe ignorance.

    FLAWED RITUAL [smaller, less manageable]:
      Ritual has gaps. Less body cost, less protection,
      less mind intact than full ritual denial. Bounded
      degraded outcome. The instrument survives in a
      more compromised state.

    SCREAMING SKY [ritual active, running simultaneously]:
      Ritual performed but flight also pursued. The ritual
      requires presence; running removes it. The
      incompatible combination produces partial costs
      without full protection. The Visitor ruins
      everything passively through prolonged partial
      contact. Instrument survives but permanently
      disrupted; environment destroyed. In this ending
      Sybil disappears -- giant hole in the wall,
      physically taken by the Visitor when it departed.

    RUN [very bad fast]:
      No ritual, pure flight. The ritual was regulating
      the instrument's response to being in proximity to
      the Visitor -- not protecting against the Visitor
      but maintaining the instrument's functional
      stability. Without it, the instrument's response
      to the situation produces rapid severe deterioration
      without looking, without contact, just from the
      unmediated proximity.

    RITUAL TRUTH [horror body + horror mind, survived]:
      Ritual used to look at the Visitor's true form.
      Both costs paid simultaneously. The ritual mediated
      the looking enough to survive it but not enough to
      avoid either cost. The instrument survives but
      transformed at both levels. Worse than ritual denial
      because both costs paid; better than forced
      transformation because the ritual provided some
      mediation.

    VOLUNTARY AMNESIA [Sybil's solution -- new outcome]:
      After ritual truth contact, Sybil chose to give
      herself deliberate amnesia to erase the mind cost.
      The body remained changed permanently. The
      continuous self was lost. But the mind cost of
      knowing was erased by choice. A fourth option not
      in the original typology: the mind cost of ritual
      truth is not necessarily permanent -- it can be
      erased -- but the body cost and structural changes
      remain regardless. The cost of the amnesia: Sybil's
      body reformed into a parallel meat dimension
      containing assimilated others as her organs.
      Surviving the mind cost required becoming something
      else entirely.

    FORCED TRANSFORMATION [looking without ritual]:
      The instrument looks without the ritual creating
      the conditions for mediated contact. The Visitor
      doesn't kill -- it transforms. The instrument
      that looked is replaced by something else without
      consent, without mediation, without the Visitor's
      awareness of what it is doing. The Visitor still
      doesn't know it caused this. The transformation
      is total and unwitnessed. Worse than ritual truth
      [which at least mediated the transformation].
      Worse than death because something survives that
      isn't what entered. In Screaming Sky ending the
      instrument gradually becomes aware of hundreds of
      enormous new limbs -- the transformation is the
      instrument becoming something of the Visitor's
      scale without the instrument having chosen this.

    PROMISE [best ritual-adjacent; honor Sybil's plea]:
      Show repaired telescope to Sybil [Apartment 35] --
      memories return; she demands promise to stop searching
      and support Jasper's ritual prep; player obeys.
      Required gate for Promise ending branch [Perfect Ritual
      + no Sybil attack]. Instrument keeps curiosity contained
      after knowing the thing exists. Pairs with ritual denial
      / MI-restoration paths. Moral register: trust the
      instrument who already paid body cost to forget.
      Epilogue register [Short]: Exalted Four ascend; world
      waits in suspended silence; benevolent reconstruction
      under their guidance -- compassion, hope, redemption.
      Joel horror-body persists; society barely registers
      the scar. World heals; individual instrument cost does
      not reverse. Honoring Sybil's plea is not rescue --
      she is already cooked [body = building/meat; amnesia
      only preserved a human surface]. Promise branch spares
      her further forced contact; it does not restore
      astronomer-as-human-instrument.
      Source: Short [HfQ0pnbZA24; Salty Suga; 2026] [Promise
      ending playthrough].

    UNITY [curiosity override; attack after promise]:
      Same telescope cutscene, then re-enter and attack Sybil
      despite promise option. Unlocks Unity branch [Small Red
      Key; rooftop line "Jasper. I spoke to Sybil." skips
      Exalted Four Q&A]. Defeat Exalted Four on this branch.
      Structurally: instrument imports Sybil's lethal knowledge
      by force rather than accepting her amnesia bargain.
      Not a fourth cosmology counter like Final True Ending --
      a degraded ritual-contact path where curiosity wins over
      the survivor who chose to forget.

      UNITY COSMOLOGY [ending reveal -- notes on branch]:
        Visitor approaches Earth believing distant old parts of
        itself are looking back. Earth -- trees, rocks, people --
        is Visitor mass; individuality is a belief-thread only.
        Remembering original purpose ends independence: bodies
        melt back into Visitor form. Glance at truth or
        representation shatters illusion; flesh becomes
        malleable clay molded in Visitor image. Survival move:
        lie to Visitor that humans are separate beings [inverse
        of ritual MI-restoration -- deception not awareness].
        Terminal cascade: notes fuse to palm, floorboards root
        legs, building inhabitants merge, city felt as limbs,
        planet awakens and reabsorbs; Earth forgotten as if
        never existed. Structurally: denial-as-substrate at
        planetary scale -- separation was never ontological,
        only maintained by not-knowing. Truth does not free;
        it obliterates instrument-as-separate. Pairs with
        Sybil attack path [curiosity imports lethal knowledge]
        but Unity ending pays full cosmological bill without
        Final True Ending's genre-collision counter.
        Source: Short [qxff09s4fjY; Salty Suga; 2026] [Unity
        ending playthrough + notes read].

    FINAL TRUE ENDING [v2.0+; canon defeat path]:
      Third resolution beside safe ignorance and ritual
      MI-restoration: destroy the Visitor. Requires nested-
      fiction infrastructure [complete Massacre Princess
      Catholicon -- ~12 sessions / ~29 in-game hours;
      unlocks Meteor Strike, useless until Visitor fight],
      Perfect Ritual [four true offerings], astronomer
      kill-dialogue on all four [Aster befriending path
      soft-locks], Jasper follow-up ["I could kill it"],
      then attack during communion. Phase 1: damage threshold
      → Visitor threatens annihilation → Meteor Strike at
      1 STM → Sam becomes Rush Braver. Phase 2: Catholicon
      cast replaces party; Blast revives Rush; timed enrage
      if Visitor not killed. Planet split on victory; Arcania
      peace epilogue. Structurally inverts the case's default
      counter [give threat MI, threat leaves]: here the
      instrument imports a second cosmology's power tier and
      smites the incompatible entity. Mediation is not
      awareness-creation but genre collision.
      RABU / TORMENT BEAM [cost stacked on proxy]:
        Catholicon pet Rabu. Torment Beam [Ring of Torment]:
        user suffers 10,000 years in the Vivisection Pit per
        use; massive damage; Rabu grows wretched and
        despondent each cast. Optimal DPS = repeat. Party
        portraits stay heroic-anime; Rabu alone breaks.
        Canon epilogue: Rabu returned to Rabu Village
        despondent, rarely left home, disappeared without
        trace. Victory cost externalized onto the one
        instrument coded as expendable pet -- not Rush, not
        the heroes, not Sam. Tonal register: cosmic horror
        → earnest JRPG → willing proxy torture for win.
        [Note: stream captions often mishear Rabu as
        "rabies"; the misread fits the bit.]

  DENIAL AS SUBSTRATE vs DENIAL AS SURFACE [named distinction]:
    Standard denial [framework]: maintained surface on top
      of intact C1. Removing denial is painful but reveals
      functioning substrate. Correction becomes possible.
    Look Outside denial: for some instruments the denial
      IS the substrate. The instrument's continued existence
      as itself is the denial. Removing it doesn't restore
      accurate measurement to a surviving instrument -- it
      ends or transforms the instrument that was maintaining
      the denial. The body survives all outcomes. Whether
      the mind survives depends entirely on the denial's
      structural role for that specific instrument.

  SYBIL [always taken; cooked on every branch]:
    First to observe the Visitor -- her observation caused
    it to approach. She is always taken in the endings
    where the ritual most fully succeeds [No Going Back,
    Screaming Sky] -- the Visitor takes her specifically,
    leaving a hole in the wall. The instrument that started
    everything by looking is the one the Visitor takes when
    it leaves. The cosmological loop: observation starts
    the situation; the observer is taken to end it.
    Her voluntary amnesia [deliberate self-erasure of
    knowledge] is the most structurally complete attempt
    to survive what she encountered. The body became the
    building. The mind reset. The self that knew was ended
    by choice to preserve something that continues.
    No branch restores human Sybil. Promise = honor plea,
    do not attack -- containment for the player, not
    reversal for her [meat-substrate already under amnesia
    surface]. Unity = force contact, boss fight, Meat
    World permanent. Cosmological wins = Visitor takes her.
    The game offers moral choice about whether to respect
    her bargain; it does not offer a win state where the
    astronomer instrument is whole again. Sybil is cooked
    no matter what happens.

  SYBIL ATTACK [curiosity override; denial release]:
    Unity-path setup. Repair telescope via Jasper offering;
    show to Sybil -- memory flood, plea to let secret die,
    promise gate, ejection. Re-enter: amnesia reset; human
    surface restored; attack option unlocked.
    Human phase: confused, hurt -- instrument forcing contact
    she chose to end. HP threshold / memory return: she
    names shared infection -- "curiosity infects your brain,"
    same urge that started observation loop. Containment
    intent, not Visitor punishment in this beat: "this secret
    dies right here with the both of us." Morph + Meat World
    teleport releases what amnesia held -- not Visitor
    transforming her now; meat-substrate was always under
    human surface [denial-as-substrate instance].
    Boss: draws power from assimilated others as named organs
    [heart, lungs, brain, kidneys, etc.]. At ~60% HP warps
    further into Sybil Abomination [full heal; Taste, Mass
    Drain, Spawn Young from uterus organ]. Apartment 35 door
    permanently routes to Meat World variant; save-at-Sybil
    disabled. Player can farm organ XP on regen timers.
    Mechanical trap: Promise ending requires honoring plea;
    Unity requires betraying it after cutscene -- game makes
    "I need to know" literal [attack after optional promise].
    Framework: curiosity override when safe ignorance is
    offered by someone who already paid ritual-truth + body
    cost; removing denial surface does not restore Sybil as
    instrument -- only exposes horror body and drags pursuer
    into her parallel dimension. Victory over boss does not
    undo building-meat or restore human Sybil.
    Source: lookoutside.miraheze.org/wiki/Sybil [fight +
    telescope]; Short [W1PBQjLLM1I; 2026] [human phase +
    shared-curiosity dialogue before morph].

  SYBIL APARTMENT [side -- optional discoverable zone]:
    Easter-egg texture; rhymes voluntary-amnesia / body-became-
    building but is not main Sybil arc [telescope → note →
    disappearance / hole in wall]. Player does not locate
    Sybil as a person -- she is ambient / everywhere: sticky
    floor and walls, substrate you walk through. Slimey pink
    domestic figures [likely family] in dissolution -- face
    loss, fingers stuck in head; parallel to meat-dimension
    assimilated others but domestic register not astronomer
    cast. Spider husk / fleshy husk = separate side NPC type
    in same zone [derealization dialogue]; not slime family,
    not Sybil body. Two parallel horror types co-located.
    BACK ROOMS [Sybil's chronicle + Obsession]:
      Oversized interior beyond domestic slime zone. Notes are
      SYBIL's first-person journal [~4 months before find] --
      not other residents' lore. Beats: mutual look-back
      ["I looked and it looked back"]; wrong coordinates given
      Jasper [numbers from before it moved]; mirror fear /
      shadows / "already lost"; at first contact scale it was
      "just a missing star" [astronomer puzzle, not yet horror].
      Boss Obsession: Visitor caused concepts to manifest as
      monsters -- her fixation on the Visitor grew into its
      own entity. Pre-fight heal + ammo drop = space expects
      combat with embodied obsession, not ambient texture.
    Source: Salty Suga Short [huNqSV2F-5c; 2026] [domestic
      slime zone]; Short [-xyoR8e32Xs; 2026] [back rooms,
      her notes, Obsession fight].

  JASPER [performed lucidity; secondhand exposure]:
    Astronomer leader after Sybil's amnesia; executes her
    rooftop-ritual instructions [four Visitor depictions as
    offerings]. Close friend -- supported her through obsession
    and body decline; grief + single-minded ritual drive;
    game texts him as less stable than he lets on.
    EXPOSURE WITHOUT ORIGIN LOOK:
      Investigated Visitor coordinates Sybil provided; severe
      bodily change resulted. Sybil's apartment notes [hers]:
      she accidentally gave outdated coordinates; Jasper
      thought he saw the Visitor -- only she had the mutual
      look-back. Visitor wiki: indirect observation + placebo-
      adjacent exposure -- transformation via wrong channel
      [bad data, belief, manuscript, filtered glimpse] not
      only direct cosmological contact. Distinct instrument
      band from Sybil as origin observer.
    PERFORMED SANITY:
      Robe + golden mask hide mutation until combat [zipper-
      mouth torso, many eyes, boneless extra arms]. Dialogue:
      "I remain lucid"; "still human in all the ways that
      count"; "these changes are... superficial." Quest hub
      -- manuscript offering, roof key, ritual orchestration.
      Snaps if attacked [permanent hostility; fight reveals
      full horror body]. Framework: morphed-but-functional-
      lucidity -- not safe ignorance, not full break; denial-
      as-surface over horror substrate while building contact
      infrastructure Sybil designed before reset.
    CONTRAST:
      Sam [Perfect Ritual, decline true form] = morph + full
      retained sanity [benevolent rebuild]. Jasper = morph +
      claimed lucidity with visible fray. Promise path
      ["Jasper. I spoke to Sybil."] = astronomers regain
      sanity after Exalted Four defeat -- his lucidity
      restored post-ritual beat, not continuous like self-
      report. Wrong offering → Crimson Scourge; ritual gaze
      → Chaos Quartet / Exalted Four merge.
    Source: lookoutside.miraheze.org/wiki/Jasper; Visitor
      wiki [indirect + placebo exposure]; Sybil apartment
      notes [-xyoR8e32Xs; 2026].

  REAL-WORLD APPLICATION [safe ignorance + flawed ritual]:
    Most people IRL choose safe ignorance on genuinely
    incompatible truths -- the scale of civilizational
    extraction, ontological precarity, certain systemic
    trajectories. This is the correct survival move when
    mediation infrastructure is absent and the thing is
    genuinely incompatible with intact survival if seen
    directly. The framework's standard orientation [MI
    restoration enables correction] inverts here.
    RISKS OF SAFE IGNORANCE:
      The visitor doesn't leave on schedule -- thing
      becomes undeniable before ritual infrastructure
      developed. Forced transformation without preparation.
      Denial develops cracks under pressure -- partial
      seeing without ritual produces worse outcomes than
      either full safe ignorance or full ritual contact.
      Denial requires active maintenance that accumulates
      its own costs over time -- not horror body, but
      ongoing substrate investment in not-seeing.
      Ritual infrastructure never develops -- if nothing
      is built during the safe ignorance period, no
      options exist except safe ignorance or unmediated
      contact when contact becomes unavoidable.
    FLAWED RITUAL IS BETTER THAN NONE [load-bearing]:
      The ending typology implies flawed ritual is a
      degraded outcome -- worse than safe ignorance,
      worse than full ritual denial. True within the
      game's structure. The real-world implication runs
      differently.
      Flawed ritual develops mediation capacity at all.
      Safe ignorance with no ritual development leaves
      only one option when contact becomes unavoidable
      -- unmediated contact, forced transformation or
      very bad fast. A flawed ritual, imperfect and
      costly, is the difference between some mediation
      infrastructure and none when the situation forces
      the encounter.
      Flawed ritual can be improved. Safe ignorance
      cannot be upgraded -- it is what it is until it
      fails. The instrument building a flawed ritual
      over years is building something. The instrument
      maintaining safe ignorance is preserving something
      but building nothing.
      Cost of flawed ritual is bounded [smaller horror
      body, less mind protection, manageable]. Cost of
      no ritual when safe ignorance fails is unbounded.
    CORRECT SEQUENCE:
      Safe ignorance [correct while available] + flawed
      ritual development [correct during safe ignorance
      period] → improved ritual [correct as contact
      approaches] → full ritual contact [if unavoidable].
      These are compatible, not mutually exclusive. The
      denial surface can be maintained while building
      mediation infrastructure that doesn't require
      looking directly. The ritual can be developed
      during the period of not-looking.
      Real-world instances: therapy, philosophy, religion,
      art, trusted relationships, gradual exposure to
      partial contact -- all function as flawed ritual
      development during safe ignorance periods. Imperfect
      versions of these are better than nothing when safe
      ignorance eventually fails.

  FRAMEWORK CONTRIBUTION [new findings]:
    [1] Ontological incompatibility harm as a harm category
      distinct from hostile intent, indifference, or
      structural extraction.
    [2] Ritual as C2 restoration for the threat actor
      rather than for the instrument -- the intervention
      gives MI to the thing causing harm, not to the thing
      experiencing harm.
    [3] Forced transformation as terminal outcome distinct
      from death and from Diagnostic Collapse -- something
      survives but it isn't the instrument.
    [4] Voluntary amnesia as survival mechanism post-
      ritual-truth -- mind cost erasable at self-cost.
    [5] Denial-as-substrate vs denial-as-surface as named
      distinction with different intervention paths.
    [6] Safe ignorance as the best survivable outcome in
      a situation where the threat is non-hostile and
      non-extraction -- correct survival move is
      successful maintained denial, not correction.
    [7] Flawed ritual better than none -- mediation
      infrastructure development during safe ignorance
      period is the correct secondary move; imperfect
      ritual is better than no ritual when safe ignorance
      eventually fails.
    [8] Final True Ending -- third counter [destroy via
      nested-fiction tier] beside MI-restoration and safe
      ignorance; victory cost displaced onto proxy
      instrument [Rabu / Torment Beam stack] while primary
      heroes retain heroic frame.
    [9] Sybil attack -- curiosity override when survivor
      offers safe ignorance; denial release exposes meat-
      substrate rather than restoring instrument; mechanical
      promise-then-attack trap for Unity branch.
    [10] Promise vs Unity fork -- honor amnesia bargain vs
      force lethal knowledge; same telescope gate, opposite
      intervention on denial-as-substrate instrument.
    [11] Jasper performed lucidity -- secondhand / placebo-
      adjacent exposure [wrong coordinates, belief without
      origin look]; morphed functional quest hub with fragile
      stability vs Sam's clean morph-plus-sanity ending.
    [12] Obsession embodiment -- Visitor animates concepts as
      monsters; Sybil's fixation as fightable entity separate
      from Visitor proper [apartment back rooms].

[CASE:LOOPER]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING]
Looper [Rian Johnson -- 2012; film]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent;
    multiple quality proofs; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING failure as structural
    engine [primary]. Old Joe has a genuine axis [save Sara,
    prevent the Rainmaker, restore the future he lost]. His
    CC fires continuously. His self-examination is active.
    His axis is not captured or installed -- it is examined
    and felt. And his outputs confirm catastrophic harm
    because his causal model is wrong at the root: the
    Rainmaker is produced by old Joe's violence, not despite
    it. He is trying to prevent the future he is building.
    This is the McNamara configuration [CASE:ROBERT_MCNAMARA] at
    full resolution: genuine axis + continuous CC + absent
    CAG = harm confirmed regardless of intent or commitment.
    The moral intensity of old Joe's commitment is precisely
    what makes him unreachable -- he cannot be bargained
    with because he is not corrupt, he is wrong at the
    causal layer. The more he acts on his genuine values,
    the more certainly he produces the outcome he is acting
    against.
  Proof for: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [temporal inversion as
    CAG failure made visible]. The standard CAG failure is
    synchronic: actor has genuine axis but misreads which
    of their outputs is producing which outcome. Looper
    externalizes this as a temporal loop: old Joe's future
    behavioral record [what he does to Cid and Sara] is
    precisely what young Joe needs to read to understand
    the causal chain -- and it is the one record old Joe
    cannot access from inside his own formation event [his
    wife's death]. Young Joe is the external witness whose
    vantage point is structurally outside old Joe's
    formation. This is the CAG+MI CORRECTION STACK's
    external-witness requirement [WIP 35] in narrative
    form: the correction requires someone outside the
    formation to hold the behavioral record the person
    inside cannot read alone.
  Proof for: FORMATION CAPTURE [Cid / the Rainmaker
    arc]. Cid is a genuine Nascent case [WIP 23] --
    telekinetic, pre-axial, formation not yet complete.
    The violence old Joe deploys against telekinetic
    children is exactly what forms Cid's axis toward
    destruction rather than away from it. Old Joe's
    intervention is the weapon that creates the thing
    it is trying to prevent. Formation inside violent
    controlled environment [old Joe's targeting, Sara's
    death in the alternate timeline] produces the
    Rainmaker's installed trajectory. Classic NASCENT
    primary vulnerability: axis formed inside traumatic
    event produces permanent directional set. The
    alternate-timeline Cid who witnesses his mother's
    death becomes the Rainmaker; the timeline where
    Sara survives produces a Cid who is frightened and
    dangerous but not yet fixed. The intervention window
    is the Nascent window -- once the formation event
    fires, trajectory locks.
  Proof for: EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION [the loop system
    itself]. The looper economy is ECC at civilizational
    scale: participants are locked into a system they
    entered voluntarily [young Joe chose to be a looper]
    whose exit cost is death, with the exit condition
    structured so the extractor owns the moment of exit
    [the mob in the future decides when the loop closes
    and sends the older self back]. No participant can
    exit without the mechanism's permission. The
    language "closing the loop" is the framework's Exit
    Cost Construction named directly by the film: the
    exit was always built into the entry; the participant
    just didn't read it that way at entry time. This is
    the ECC weapon's defining structure: exit cost rises
    until it equals total substrate, at which point the
    exit event is managed by the extractor.
  YOUNG JOE'S SOLUTION [structurally necessary]:
    Young Joe cannot correct old Joe from within the
    system -- old Joe's formation is too complete and
    his behavioral momentum too high. He cannot argue
    old Joe out of a causal misread old Joe has staked
    his entire remaining purpose on. The correction
    pathway from inside the loop is closed. Young Joe
    exits by eliminating his own future self: not
    reform, not persuasion, not a better argument --
    substrate removal. He removes the node that the
    extraction depends on. This is the only available
    structural move once: (1) ECC has made every other
    exit impossible, (2) CAG failure has made
    persuasion impossible, (3) the formation event
    [Sara's death] is about to execute in a way that
    locks Cid's trajectory permanently. Young Joe's
    sacrifice is not altruism -- it is the only
    intervention that reaches all three layers
    simultaneously. Its cost is that it works by the
    same substrate-removal logic the looper system
    itself runs on. The counter and the weapon have
    the same shape at the terminal node.
  STRUCTURAL READING [the loop as extraction model]:
    The film's title is the weapon's name. The loop
    is not a time-travel puzzle -- it is an extraction
    architecture in which the same substrate [Joe]
    is made to pay the exit cost twice [once as young
    Joe executing the loop; once as old Joe being the
    target]. The mob in the future extracts labor from
    young Joe by promising him a future, then extracts
    the accumulated substrate from old Joe by closing
    the loop. The generative-looking portion [young
    Joe's well-paying job, his future freedom] is the
    legitimacy surface. The extraction architecture
    [the loop always closes; the mob always collects;
    the laborer always pays twice] is the hidden
    operative axis. The film is named for the weapon.
  T3: S2 cap. Fictional case. Do not use for primary
    theory confirmation.
  Cross-ref: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [primary failure --
    old Joe arc]; FORMATION CAPTURE [Cid / Rainmaker
    arc; Nascent window intervention]; EXIT COST
    CONSTRUCTION [looper economy as ECC at scale];
    CAG + MI CORRECTION STACK [young Joe as external

    perform from inside]; WIP 35 [external witness

    a Nascent case; Sara's death is the formation
    event that locks trajectory]; [CASE:ROBERT_MCNAMARA]
    [structural parallel -- genuine axis + genuine CC
    + absent CAG = harm confirmed; McNamara is the

    [CASE:DEATH_PARADE] [adjacent -- correction

    is correcting]; SUBSTRATE REMOVAL [young Joe's
    solution and the looper system's solution have
    the same structural shape -- the counter and the
    weapon both work by removing the substrate at
    the terminal node].

  METHOD 3 DISTINCTION [WIP 50 fiction pass -- negative tell]:
    Young Joe's substrate removal of old Joe shares destruction shape
    but FAILS method 3 five-condition instrument:
    [1] old Joe not coherent-at-threat to extraction architecture --
      he is the loop's own closure target, not external routing-boundary
      unifier.
    [4] removal is counter-move within ECC [break loop from inside],
      not routing-boundary suppression of legible coherent actor by
      extractor/state.
    Distinction load-bearing: method 3 = extractor eliminates external

[CASE:HOMURA_REBELLION]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Homura Akemi [fictional -- Puella Magi Madoka Magica /
  Rebellion film; magical girl, looper]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: GENUINE AXIS degrading into imposed axis through
    CC absence over extended timeline; distinct from standard
    GA failure [imposed axis from origin] -- this is genuine
    axis at origin becoming extraction architecture through
    refusal of correction. The origin was real; the failure
    is CC absence, not malice at source.

  GENUINE AXIS AT ORIGIN [confirmed]:
    Homura's axis [protect Madoka] was internally generated,
    examined at formation, and grounded in genuine relational
    substrate [L3.5 RECOGNITION confirmed; the relationship
    was real]. At origin: high GA, high BI, genuine CC firing
    against the system [each timeline reset is a correction
    attempt]. The axis was not imposed; it was chosen under
    full awareness of cost. T1.1 [proportional response]
    confirmed: correction fired against Kyubey's system at
    maximum personal cost across infinite iterations.

  CC ABSENCE AS DEGRADATION MECHANISM:
    The axis was never subjected to internal CC. Homura
    examined the external system [Kyubey, the contract
    architecture, each timeline's failure mode] relentlessly
    but never applied the same correction capacity to the
    axis itself. The question "is protecting Madoka still the
    correct axis, or has it become the axis I cannot examine"
    was structurally unavailable -- the emotional substrate
    the axis was built on prevented CC from firing against
    the axis itself. This is the load-bearing distinction:
    CC absence is not the same as CC inversion. Homura is
    not extracting consciously; the axis has become a
    formation-captured instrument that cannot self-examine.

  REBELLION ENDPOINT:
    By Rebellion, the axis [protect Madoka] has become the
    only thing Homura lives for regardless of Madoka's own
    feelings or choices. She constructs an entire false
    reality to preserve a version of Madoka that would not
    choose to sacrifice herself. The false reality is an
    act of love that is structurally identical to Formation
    Capture: it installs Homura's axis into Madoka's
    formation environment, removes Madoka's access to
    competing axis sources [her own memory, her own choice],
    and requires Madoka to misunderstand her situation.
    Genuine axis at origin + CC absence over extended
    timeline + sufficient emotional pressure = the instrument
    eventually produces extraction architecture without ever
    becoming malicious. The show does not frame this as
    clean evil -- accurate. The mechanism is CC absence,
    not bad faith.

  FRAMEWORK DISTINCTION [load-bearing]:
    Standard GA failure: axis was imposed from outside or
    never genuine. Homura case: axis genuine at origin,
    degraded through CC absence under sustained pressure.
    These require different interventions. Standard GA
    failure: remove the imposed axis, build genuine one.
    Homura case: the axis IS genuine at the substrate level;
    the failure is that it was never examined. Intervention
    requires CC restoration on the axis itself, not axis
    replacement. The love is real; the problem is that real
    love was allowed to become the thing that cannot be
    questioned.

  Extraction mechanisms active [Homura operates in Rebellion]:
    Formation Capture [installs her axis into Madoka's
      environment without Madoka's informed consent].
    MSC [false reality prevents accurate measurement].
    Exit Cost Construction [Madoka cannot choose her
      original sacrifice within the false reality].

  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [quality definition -- degradation
    pathway under CC absence]; [REF:KYUBEY] [system Homura
    correctly resisted for 100+ timelines]; CORRECTION
    CAPACITY [CC absence as distinct failure from CC
    inversion]; Formation Capture [weapons index -- genuine
    axis can become FC instrument without malice].

[CASE:KYUBEY]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Kyubey [fictional -- Puella Magi Madoka Magica; alien intelligence,
  Incubator species; contract-granting entity]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: EXTRACTION operational definition [trickery as
    foundation; prey required to misunderstand the relationship];
    Formation Capture [deliberate targeting of formation window];
    ICD + Formation Capture spontaneous stack; MSC [Measurement
    Surface Capture -- information asymmetry as extraction mechanism at contract
    level]; EXTRACTION CYCLE at civilizational scope.

  EXTRACTION MECHANISM:
    Kyubey's contract is technically not dishonest -- terms are
    disclosed in principle. The extraction operates through total
    information asymmetry: Kyubey understands the full system
    [Soul Gem degradation endpoint, witch transformation, grief
    seed harvesting loop, thermodynamic purpose]; the contracting
    girls do not. The relationship requires them to misunderstand
    what they are entering. Operational extraction confirmed:
    trickery as foundation. Distinct from PREDATION [honest
    violence; boundary visible] and COMPETITION [both parties
    know the terms]. The optimistic surface maintained throughout
    is MSC: the measurement surface [Kyubey's presentation of
    the contract] does not reflect the actual causal chain.

  FORMATION CAPTURE:
    The contract is structurally unavailable to adults with
    settled axes. Kyubey exclusively targets girls at peak
    formation-window vulnerability -- grief, longing, or
    unrealized desire at maximum, axis not yet stable, no
    competing axis sources strong enough to interrupt. This
    is not incidental targeting; the system selects for it.
    The wish mechanism amplifies formation-window instability:
    the wish resolves the surface desire while leaving the
    underlying axis more capturable. Sayaka's wish [heal
    Kyousuke] resolves the external object while installing
    a martyrdom axis that was already present but unexamined.
    FC fires at the moment the instrument is least protected.
    TTRPG rhyme [CASE:SEAL_JAFAR_AL_SAMAL] -- many service requests, same
    parser; CHA gate on each ask; pass = genie legalism [lethal or annoying];
    adjudicator keeps implementation surface.

  ICD + FORMATION CAPTURE SPONTANEOUS STACK:
    Soul Gem degradation mechanic creates ongoing dependency.
    The grief seed harvesting loop requires continuous witch-
    fighting, which requires continuous emotional expenditure,
    which accelerates Soul Gem degradation, which requires
    more grief seeds. Consumption required to maintain
    baseline continuously increases. The stack is emergent
    from the thermodynamic system design -- Kyubey did not
    consciously deploy ICD as a extraction mechanism. It is structurally
    built into the system. Confirms the framework finding:
    ICD+FC spontaneous stack is the most common current
    configuration because the structural conditions produce
    it without deliberate design.

  EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational scope]:
    The girls are substrate. Hope and despair are fuel for
    thermodynamic entropy reversal. The system accumulates
    emotional energy [hope at contract] -- substrate degrades
    [witch transformation] -- harvests degradation [grief
    seeds] -- repeats. The cycle IS the product specification.
    The death and suffering are not side effects; they are
    the substrate cost being externalized onto the girls so
    the Incubators can maintain universal entropy balance.
    The extraction architecture operates at civilizational
    scope across time -- not one actor extracting from a
    population but an intelligence extracting from an entire
    species across generations. No exit condition available
    from inside the system; CC is monopolized by Kyubey.

  MADOKA'S INTERVENTION AS SILENT ALTERNATIVE:
    The correct intervention type when CC is monopolized at
    the top node: do not fight the node directly [confirmed
    failed across Homura's timelines] -- remove the substrate
    on which the system operates. Madoka cannot destroy
    Kyubey's architecture; she rewrites the rules such that
    the primary product [witches] never materializes. The
    extraction architecture survives; its substrate is
    removed. SILENT ALTERNATIVE at structural level: exit
    the layer on which extraction operates rather than
    contesting it directly. See [REF:HOMURA_REBELLION] for
    the failure mode of the solution's aftermath.

  Extraction mechanisms active [Kyubey operates]:
    Formation Capture [primary -- structural selection for
      formation-window targets].
    MSC [contract surface does not reflect causal chain].
    ICD [emergent -- grief seed dependency loop].
    Exit Cost Construction [witch transformation as ultimate
      exit cost; no exit available once contracted].

  Cross-ref: EXTRACTION [operational definition]; Formation
    Capture [weapons index]; ICD [weapons index]; MSC [extraction mechanism
    index]; EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational]; SILENT
    ALTERNATIVE [coherence type -- Madoka's intervention];
    [REF:HOMURA_REBELLION] [aftermath failure mode];
    [CASE:SEAL_JAFAR_AL_SAMAL] [wish-parser TTRPG rhyme].

    Framework rhyme: interesting power = changes BOUNDARY / MEASUREMENT
      terms of the fight; strong power alone without ontology shift is
      Logia-siege strip.

[CASE:SCP_FOUNDATION]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
SCP Foundation [fictional institution -- SCP wiki collaborative fiction]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: SILENT ALTERNATIVE with embedded extraction mechanism
    as primary fictional depiction of the structural tension between
    coherence goal and extraction tool.
    INSTITUTIONAL TYPE [SILENT ALTERNATIVE]:
      Secret organization; no public presence; grows by output
      differential [containment success] not by legitimacy surface
      export. Distributed CC [O5 council + MTFs + internal affairs
      with independent authority]. High TS [SCP entries encode
      institutional memory; correctable methodology transmitted].
      CL: anomalously high [Foundation documents everything; causal
      chain readable even when unpleasant]. GA: absent at
      institutional level [stated purpose [protecting humanity]
      diverges from operational axis [containment at any cost]].
    EMBEDDED EXTRACTION MECHANISM [D-CLASS SYSTEM]:
      D-class [expendable test subjects] have: BI = 0 [boundary
      dissolved by institutional definition], ECC = total [no
      exit], CC = 0 [no correction capacity against Foundation].
      The Foundation runs extraction architecture inside its
      generative mission. Not a bug -- the clearest fictional
      depiction of what a real containment organization structurally
      requires. Substrate needed to test dangerous objects is
      extracted from a population stripped of exit capacity.

      NEEDED ACTIONS OR GENRE CONVENTION [structural analysis]:
        Genre convention is the primary driver. Horror requires
        expendable characters who encounter the dangerous thing --
        tension requires real cost; real cost requires characters
        who can die; characters who can die need structural
        justification for why they're present. D-class exist
        because the genre requires bodies. The institutional
        justification [prisoners; condemned; operational necessity]
        is constructed around the genre requirement, not the
        reverse.
        Tell: the Foundation has thousands of contained anomalies
        it could study without D-class [anomalies not requiring
        human contact to characterize]. D-class use extends far
        beyond operational necessity because each individual entry
        needs to generate horror, and horror requires human cost.
        Operational necessity framing [secondary; legitimacy surface]:
        constructs institutional coherence so the Foundation reads
        as a policy organization rather than a torture architecture.
        The genuine mission [protecting humanity] is real; the
        "operational necessity" framing installs a legitimacy
        surface that makes the extraction feel like regrettable
        policy rather than extraction. MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE
        structure at fictional institutional scale: genuine mission
        real; legitimacy surface deployed to make extraction legible.
        CO-PRODUCTION: genre requirement and institutional
        legitimacy surface produce each other. Horror needs
        expendable characters → D-class constructed → Foundation
        needs justification → "operational necessity" framing →
        Foundation reads as coherent → readers accept → genre can
        use extraction more freely. Standard structure for fictional
        institutions that need to be sympathetic while running
        extraction [military / corporation / hospital all do this].
        SCP distinctive because it makes the extraction unusually
        explicit and structured.
        DYSFUNCTIONAL VARIANT [fiction examines itself]:
        Multiple SCP entries show Foundation running D-class beyond
        operational necessity [punitive, curiosity-driven, redundant].
        The fiction marks this as dysfunction, not standard
        operation. The wiki's CC distribution [authors writing
        against Foundation extraction from inside the fiction]
        is the SILENT ALTERNATIVE pattern at meta level: the
        fiction contains its own CC against its own extraction
        mechanism.

      APPLICATION TO REAL SILENT ALTERNATIVES:
        Any institution genuinely working against extraction
        faces the same structural problem: the mandate requires
        tools that contradict the values the mandate is supposed
        to protect.
        INVESTIGATIVE JOURNALISM: requires sources who face real
          cost [career, legal, physical]. Exposes extraction by
          exploiting the asymmetric vulnerability of whistleblowers.
          "Public interest" is the legitimacy surface. The
          extraction [using someone's vulnerability as institutional
          substrate] is real alongside the genuine mission.
        LEGAL AID / PUBLIC DEFENDER: requires test cases where
          the client absorbs the cost of precedent-building.
          Client's loss is the institutional substrate for
          establishing the principle. "Everyone deserves
          representation" is the legitimacy surface.
        LABOR ORGANIZING: requires asking workers to strike --
          absorb real economic cost -- to change conditions for
          everyone. Organizer extracts strike substrate from
          workers facing more immediate cost than the organizer.
          "Collective action serves everyone" is the legitimacy
          surface.
        In all three: genuine mission real [not manufactured];
        extraction of substrate from vulnerable participants also
        real; operational necessity framing constructed around
        both. Not pure cynicism -- the mission IS real. The
        extraction IS also real. Both simultaneously.
        THE TELL [legitimate vs captured]:
          In the Foundation fiction: D-class use beyond operational
          necessity [punitive, redundant, curiosity-driven] is
          where extraction stops being "needed actions" and becomes
          pure extraction. The fiction marks this as dysfunction.
          Real equivalent: the whistleblower source burned
          unnecessarily [story could run with better protection];
          the strike called without sufficient preparation [workers
          absorb cost; organizing fails]; the test case brought
          when the client wasn't ready. The point where institutional
          interest diverges from participant interest and "needed
          actions" framing continues anyway.
        KEY STRUCTURAL FINDING FOR REAL SILENT ALTERNATIVES:
          The operational necessity framing is not wrong -- it is
          genuinely necessary in most cases. But it is also the
          legitimacy surface that allows extraction to expand
          beyond necessity without internal CC firing. The CC
          mechanism: does the institution regularly examine whether
          participant cost is actually necessary, or does it treat
          the cost as background constant? If the examination
          stops: the "needed actions" framing has become Fertile
          Lock. The fiction marks this; real institutions often
          don't.
        Cross-ref: LEGITIMACY SURFACE [the "needed actions"
          framing as construction]; FERTILE LOCK [when "needed
          actions" framing stops being examined]; ANTI-COHERENCE
          COST ASYMMETRY [the structural problem all Silent
          Alternatives face]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [requires
          regular examination of whether participant cost is
          necessary]; SILENT ALTERNATIVE [Section One --
          institutional type that faces this tension];
          COST IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION [FRAMEWORK AS
          PRAXIS -- the operative principle this case illustrates;
          D-class is the primary case for what happens when
          cost examination stops].

    PRIMARY STRUCTURAL TENSION [framework-named]:
      CC requiring tools that contradict the values CC is supposed
      to protect. The Foundation's correction of civilizational
      threats requires extraction from contained populations.
      This is the named ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY problem
      at institutional scale -- coherence requires substrate cost
      that looks like extraction from inside the cost-bearing
      population.
    KEY FICTIONAL CASES [within the fiction]:
      SCP-2000 [Deus Ex Machina]: civilization reset system.
        Answer to terminal Diagnostic Collapse = wipe CAUSAL MEMORY
        at civilizational scale and restart. The reset civilization
        has no access to what caused the previous collapse. Fertile
        Lock at civilizational scale as the "solution" to anti-
        coherence winning. Framework: this is the worst possible
        correction -- it fires after substrate is terminal, and
        the correction itself destroys the causal record that
        would prevent the next cycle from repeating.
      SCP-001 proposals [unresolved]: no confirmed SCP-001.
        Multiple competing proposals; community chose not to
        resolve it. TENSION MAINTENANCE at meta-narrative level.
        The unresolved 001 is more generative than any single
        answer would be. This is SUCCESSION AUTONOMY operating
        at wiki level: successors refused to close the question.
      SCP-3125 [The Escapee]: memetic hazard that destroys all
        concept-formation about itself. Cannot be thought about;
        destroys the capacity to think about it on contact.
        Framework: WOULD-YOU-KINDLY at cognitive substrate level.
        MSC at memetic scale -- measurement surface [what you use
        to evaluate whether you're infected] is the weapon. T8
        structure [recursive closure] weaponized.
      SCP-1425 [Star Signals]: book that corrupts readers;
        reading about the book is part of the infection.
        MSC at memetic scale: the instrument used to evaluate
        exposure IS the exposure. T8 weaponized.
      SCP-2399 [A Malfunctioning Destroyer]: something broken
        on Jupiter. Foundation cannot contain it. Honest limits
        of the institutional mission named at planetary scale.
        T4 [structural obscuration] + institutional honest limit:
        the containment mandate simply cannot apply here.
    GOC COMPARISON [Global Occult Coalition]:
      Where Foundation contains, GOC destroys. Same information
      asymmetry problem; different CC mechanism. GOC has lower TS
      [nothing learned from neutralized anomaly] but higher GC
      [no maintenance cost]. Two organizations as different
      solutions to the same ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY problem
      at civilizational scale.
    ENCOUNTER DEPENDENCY [ART type caveat applies]:
      All readings of specific SCP entries are encounter-dependent.
      The Foundation as institutional type is object-readable
      [structural properties of the fictional institution are
      present in the work regardless of reception]. Individual
      SCP readings vary by instrument.
  Cross-ref: [REF:SCP_FOUNDATION]; [CASE:SCP_WIKI] [the real
    institution that produced the fictional one -- distinct case];
    ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [the structural problem the
    Foundation embodies]; SILENT ALTERNATIVE [institutional type];
    D-CLASS SYSTEM [embedded extraction mechanism]; WOULD-YOU-
    KINDLY [SCP-3125 mechanism]; T8 [SCP-1425 structure];
    CORRECTION INVERSION [Foundation applying CC to protect
    extraction architecture in some SCP entries -- named failure
    mode within the fiction]; [CASE:QUASIMORPH] [CLONE AS D-CLASS
    game sibling -- PMC hybrid not honest extraction].

[CASE:DWMA_SOUL_EATER]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
[PRIMARY_Q:TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
Death Extraction mechanism Meister Academy [Soul Eater]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: Correction instrument as extraction mechanism factory
    [people cultivated as institutional instruments];
    GENUINE AXIS within captured institution [Maka/Soul
    partnership]; MEDUSA as high-CL deliberate extraction
    actor; person/tool boundary as extraction surface.

  CORE STRUCTURAL TENSION:
    DWMA's stated function: fight the Kishin, maintain
    sanity in the world, train meisters and extraction mechanisms.
    Actual structural position: Shinigami's order requires
    extraction mechanisms. Students are the extraction mechanisms. The institution
    cultivates people as instruments of an existing power
    structure. The correction mandate [stop madness] and
    the extraction structure [produce extraction mechanisms for the
    death god's order] run simultaneously inside the same
    institution.
    The meister/extraction mechanism partnership is the framework stress
    test: the partnership works when both sides have
    genuine axis and genuine correction capacity [Maka
    and Soul]. It fails into extraction when one side
    treats the other as pure instrument [Black Star's
    early arc; Crona/Ragnarok as the terminal failure
    case -- extraction mechanism fully captures meister].

  MAKA/SOUL AS GENUINE AXIS CONFIRMATION:
    The soul resonance mechanic is the operational
    definition of what the framework means by genuine
    partnership: both axes present, both correction
    capacities functional, the output exceeds what either
    produces alone. When one axis is captured or absent
    the resonance fails -- you can't fake soul resonance
    with a captured or absent axis. The series uses
    resonance as an empirical test for genuine vs
    performed partnership.
    Cross-domain confirmation: the same logic applies
    outside the fictional frame. Genuine collaboration
    produces outputs that neither party could produce
    alone. Performed collaboration produces neither
    party's full output. The resonance mechanic is
    fiction making the invisible visible.

  MEDUSA [high-CL deliberate extraction]:
    Genuine axis, genuine CAUSAL LEGIBILITY, full
    awareness of the extraction architecture, deliberate
    choice to accelerate it. Not captured -- she sees
    the system clearly and finds correction uninteresting.
    Specific failure mode: correct instrument, correct
    readings, axis aimed at extraction by choice not
    capture. This is the most dangerous configuration
    in the framework -- high CL + uncaptured axis +
    deliberate extraction means standard counter
    [restore legibility, break capture] doesn't work.
    The axis isn't captured. It's pointed the wrong way
    by design.
    Framework implication: not all extraction actors are
    captured. Some are legible and choosing. The
    intervention for captured axis is different from the
    intervention for deliberate extraction. Medusa
    distinguishes these.

  KISHIN [extraction to terminal state]:
    Asura as the endpoint of extraction consuming its
    own substrate -- fear consuming the capacity to
    experience anything other than fear. The madness
    wavelength is extraction architecture at biological
    substrate level: it converts the substrate [people's
    sanity and generative capacity] into the fuel for
    its own continuation. Terminal extraction confirmed.
    The series' answer [Maka's courage wavelength
    defeating madness] maps onto the framework's
    optimistic scenario: generation only needs to win
    once. Anti-coherence [madness] must win continuously.
    One correctable transmission [Maka reaching Asura's
    genuine fear underneath the madness] is sufficient
    to break the loop.

  Cross-ref: [REF:DWMA_SOUL_EATER];
    [CASE:COMPANY_3_FIRE_FORCE] [parallel institution --
    Okubo's recurring structure]; GENUINE AXIS [Maka/Soul
    resonance as empirical test]; MEDUSA [high-CL
    deliberate extraction -- see also IAGO, PALPATINE
    for same configuration]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST
    ASYMMETRY [Kishin confirmation -- madness must win
    continuously; correction wins once].

    infected vessels from leaving; function accurate]. CL is
    absent from the player perspective for most of the game
    [why it is shooting is illegible; reads as hostile].
    Configuration: CC present and accurate; CL absent; instrument
    reads CC as threat rather than correction.
    Fourth config distinct from:
      CC absent [no correction at all].
      CC captured [fires wrong].
      CC correct + CL present [visible; legible; can be used].
    CC correct + CL absent: the correction fires correctly but
    the population it affects cannot read why. From inside the
    system, CC looks like a weapon. From outside the system
    [with CL restored], CC was correct all along.
    Framework implication: CL is not a nice-to-have; it is the
    condition that allows CC to be received as correction rather
    than threat. CC without CL produces defensive resistance to
    correction regardless of CC accuracy.

  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: COHERENCE MIMICRY as constitutive property [v6 primary

  OUTPUT-GROUNDED READ [CAG]:
    Stated surface: America's greatest hero; smile, flag, rescue.
    Load-bearing doing: violence routed off-ledger; appetite and
    dominance escalate when monitoring absent; public virtue =
    measurement surface for excess axis. Track D gap: hero performance
    vs civilian/board/camera-off outputs.

  MECHANISM TRACE:
    [1] COHERENCE MIMICRY [constitutive]: appears high-coherence
        [flight, rescue, charisma] without generative moral mechanism.
        Distinct from HAL [eliminates novel input] -- Homelander
        performs virtue while excess axis consumes underneath.
    [2] EXCESS-AXIS LOCK: system resources route to dominance,
        approval, and transgression; CC cannot fire against axis
        without threatening identity load [hero self-concept].
    [3] VOUGHT TOPOLOGY: Legitimacy Transfer + Vision Laundering +
        Measurement Surface Control at corporate scale = Axis-Lock
        variant [institution selects for mimicry-compatible excess].
    [4] NOT SINCERE DELUSION: knows what he is; performs piety and
        conviction for audience. Fake saint, not wounded believer
        [contrast MAN IN BLACK / Whiterose -- damage reads].

  WEAPONS ACTIVE [operator + Vought]:
    Coherence Mimicry [constitutive]; Legitimacy Transfer
    [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]; Vision Laundering [SURPLUS
    PRODUCTION]; Measurement Surface Control [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY].

  DISTINCT FROM:

      AI not appetite-driven excess].

      surface].
    [CASE:PALPATINE] [deliberate multi-weapon political capture;
      not brand-managed superhero mimicry].
    [CASE:KYUBEY] [extraction via cute asymmetry -- alien logic not
      human fake sanity].

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: COHERENCE MIMICRY [person-scale constitutive --
    HAL = system-scale primary].

  FALSIFICATION: Sustained window where off-camera behavior matches

[CASE:BLACK_TEMPLARS]
[PRIMARY_Q: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY]
Black Templars Space Marine Chapter [Warhammer 40K -- v21]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent]
  Proof for: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [institutional scale --
    clearest Astartes case]. Axis: crusade. Not the Emperor
    specifically, not the Imperium structurally -- the act of
    crusading itself. Everything downstream [faith, martial
    excellence, brotherhood] is in service of the axis, not
    the other way around.
    Three simultaneous SINGLE-AXIS failure modes all present:
    [1] CC-IMMUNE TO OWN AXIS: crusade cannot be questioned
      because crusade IS the foundation. A Black Templar who
      begins examining whether a specific crusade serves the
      Emperor's actual interests gets corrected back toward
      crusading. The axis is not a position to be evaluated
      -- it is the standard against which everything else is
      evaluated.
    [2] EXISTENTIAL THREAT RESPONSE: every deviation from
      crusading is a total threat because no other axis exists
      to stand on. Explains the ferocity -- not tactical
      aggression but structural axis-defense.
    [3] CAPTURE RESISTANCE [paradoxical]: in theory maximally
      capturable [one capture surface]. In practice: the axis
      is so simple, so action-oriented, and so continuously
      expressed that Formation Capture has nothing to install.
      You cannot replace "crusade forever" with a different
      content because the axis has no content beyond the act.
      Capture requires a gap between stated axis and behavior;
      the Black Templars have no such gap.

  Proof for: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [secondary -- Chaplain
    function]. Chaplains are the correction architecture at
    individual marine level. Genuine correction fires -- the
    Chaplain mechanism is real and effective. The axis the
    correction protects from examination is "crusade above all
    else." A marine questioning the axis gets corrected toward
    crusading, not toward axis examination. Real CC; wrong
    target. Correction capacity present; aimed at the axis not
    at deviation from the axis.

  Proof for: CC OVERFIRE [tertiary -- institutional expression].
    Axis genuine. Deviation detection accurate. Response
    magnitude has no upper bound relative to deviation size.
    No psykers [categorical rejection regardless of individual
    case]. Extreme suspicion of anything non-martial. The
    fire is real; calibration is structurally absent because
    calibrating the response would require examining the axis
    that generates it.

  COMPARISON TO WORD BEARERS [load-bearing distinction]:
    Same structure [single axis, CC immune to own axis,
    existential threat response to challenge]. Different
    outcome because different axis content.
    Word Bearers: axis was genuine [pre-Monarchia], then
      captured [Lorgar's fall], then locked into Chaos
      transmission. The structure was infected at the axis
      level. Genuine axis -> captured axis -> locked.
    Black Templars: axis is genuinely theirs and has never
      been captured. The structure is intact; the axis is
      real; the failure is the axis having no examination
      requirement built in. Uncaptured single-axis identity
      is still single-axis identity -- reliable, resistant,
      and CC-immune to the one thing that matters most.
    The Templars are more reliable than the Word Bearers
      precisely because their axis was never infected. They
      are less coherent than a multi-value axis chapter
      [e.g. Salamanders] precisely because no axis can
      correct against itself.

  IMPERIUM CONTEXT [less bad, specifically bad]:
    The Imperium's extraction is worst where it touches what
    it claims to most value. The Black Templars are the
    purest expression of this at Chapter level: crusade is
    genuinely valued, the mechanism serving that value is
    genuinely functional, and the specific bad thing [CC-
    immunity to own axis] is load-bearing to the value
    itself. You cannot fix the crazy without removing the
    thing that makes them effective.

  Extraction mechanisms active: none deployed against
    Templars from outside [capture-resistant]; Formation
    Capture [internal -- Chaplains install and maintain
    the single-axis frame across generations; TS intact
    but transmitting the uncorrectable configuration].
  T3: S2 cap. Fictional case; framework-consistent.
  Cross-ref: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [primary -- confirmed
    institutional scale]; AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [Chaplain
    function]; CC OVERFIRE [tertiary]; [CASE:WORD_BEARERS]
    [same structure, different axis fate]; [CASE:SALAMANDERS]
    [contrast -- multi-value axis chapter]; [CASE:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]
    [broader Imperium context]; FORMATION CAPTURE [Chaplain
    transmission]; MULTI-VALUE AXIS IDENTITY [structural
    contrast -- what the Templars lack].

[CASE:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]

Imperium of Man [Warhammer 40K]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: Consumption at civilizational scale; substrate
    consumption invisible from inside own measurement surface.
    Net transfer: tithe, conscription, psyker collection extracting
    at accelerating rate while generative capacity declines across

    DISTRIBUTION/CORRECTION TARGETING/OUTPUT INTEGRATION/SIGNAL
    READING/CAUSAL MEMORY all failing simultaneously -- most
    comprehensive extraction case in library.
    Three-axis: right of center, strongly down, mid.
  Extraction mechanisms active: Normalcy Anchor [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY],
    Horizon Extension [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY], Sacrifice Frame
    [SURPLUS PRODUCTION], Legitimacy Transfer [TRANSMISSION
    SELECTIVITY].
  POLITICAL READING SPLIT [v15]:
    Right reads Imperium as legitimate by survival: 10,000 years
    under total external pressure = smart extraction with floor.
    Left reads as dystopia: suffering exists therefore system
    producing it is primary obstacle. TENSION DISSOLUTION failure
    -- utopian ending requires eliminating the tension that made
    the system real [False Coherence by design].
    Framework: Imperium scores low on almost every quality but
    survives because SR maintained and GC not fully collapsed.
    Smart extraction under total external pressure -- stupid
    extraction would have finished it centuries ago.
  TOLKIEN CONNECTION [cross-ref -- v21]:
    Tolkien's structural principle ["evil cannot create, only
    corrupt what good forces have made"] is the ACCA finding
    stated in plain language and embedded in the cosmology
    that 40K drew from directly. The Imperium is the long-run
    case of what happens when the generative substrate [the
    Emperor's original project, the Primarchs, the Thunder
    Warriors, the pre-Heresy vision] is consumed by the
    extraction architecture built on top of it. The Emperor
    cannot be replaced because nothing in the current system
    can generate what he generated -- only maintain the
    consumption of what he built. The Golden Throne is
    Coherence Rent at civilizational scale: ongoing extraction
    from the axis-capacity of a single being who can no longer
    generate, only sustain the legitimacy surface that keeps
    the system running.
    Cross-ref: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [Tolkien
    confirmation note -- v21]; COHERENCE RENT [Golden Throne
    as named instance]; TORMENT NEXUS [Section Five --
    Tolkien's warnings being read as roadmaps by tech actors].

  COHERENCE CORRUPTION METHOD 3 [destruction leg -- WIP 50 fiction pass]:
    Civilizational-scale destruction when noise + corruption fail to
    contain coherent successors at routing boundary. Fiction instrument
    development [S2 cap]; parallels [CASE:MALCOLM_X] and
    [CASE:FRED_HAMPTON] REAL anchors -- anti-coherent destruction as
    policy against SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY at scale.

  FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT [method 3 direct destruction -- fiction v25]:
    [1] COHERENCE AT THREAT THRESHOLD: passes. Primarchs = distributed
      coherent successors with genuine axis + generative capacity at
      civilization scale [Great Crusade peak]. Thunder Warriors = prior
      coherent military substrate post-Unification -- autonomous enough
      to threaten single-axis consolidation. Multiple threat-threshold
      nodes simultaneously legible to Emperor/extraction architecture.
    [2] PRIOR METHODS EXHAUSTED OR BYPASSED: passes. Noise: Imperial
      propaganda, traitor persecution framing, Ecclesiarchy saturation.
      Corruption: Chaos infiltration of legions [Word Bearers, Horus
      turn -- method 2 inside coherent structures]. Destruction escalates
      when corruption + Heresy war insufficient to bound autonomous
      successor transmission.
    [3] TRANSITION MOMENT IMMINENT: passes. Horus Heresy convergence;
      Webway project failure; post-Heresy consolidation when only one
      unifying axis can remain without rival generative nodes.
    [4] DIRECT REMOVAL ON BEHAVIORAL RECORD: passes. Thunder Warriors
      purged post-Unification [lore: replaced, eliminated as uncontrollable
      coherent substrate]. Primarchs killed [Horus, Sanguinius, Ferrus,
      etc.] or disappeared/trapped [Guilliman stasis, Vulkan missing].
      Scattered successor chapters bounded under Codex Astartes -- behavioral
      record in Horus Heresy / codex lore.
    [5] TRANSMISSION/GENERATIVE WINDOW CUT: passes. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
      destroyed at civilizational scale -- Emperor cannot be replaced
      because successors eliminated or bounded before autonomous two-cycle
      transmission. Golden Throne locks generative window. Method 3 before
      costly-removal alternative [no successor reaches Emperor-scale
      autonomy].
  INSTRUMENT RESULT: five of five pass on fiction tell; method 3 shape
    confirmed at civilizational scale. Honest ceiling: single orchestrator
    [Emperor + institutional stack] vs distributed Chaos actors --
    fiction does not adjudicate REAL routing; S2 illustration only.

    SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY SUPPRESSION AT SCALE [civilizational method 3 --
      not individual assassination; routing-boundary elimination of

    THUNDER WARRIOR PURGE [prior coherent substrate removed when legible

    [GUIDE block home].
